  // Production steps of ECMA-262, Edition 6, 22.1.2.1
  if (!Array.from) {
    Array.from = (function () {
      var toStr = Object.prototype.toString;
      var isCallable = function (fn) {
        return typeof fn === 'function' || toStr.call(fn) === '[object Function]';
      };
      var toInteger = function (value) {
        var number = Number(value);
        if (isNaN(number)) { return 0; }
        if (number === 0 || !isFinite(number)) { return number; }
        return (number > 0 ? 1 : -1) * Math.floor(Math.abs(number));
      };
      var maxSafeInteger = Math.pow(2, 53) - 1;
      var toLength = function (value) {
        var len = toInteger(value);
        return Math.min(Math.max(len, 0), maxSafeInteger);
      };

      // The length property of the from method is 1.
      return function from(arrayLike/*, mapFn, thisArg */) {
        // 1. Let C be the this value.
        var C = this;

        // 2. Let items be ToObject(arrayLike).
        var items = Object(arrayLike);

        // 3. ReturnIfAbrupt(items).
        if (arrayLike == null) {
          throw new TypeError('Array.from requires an array-like object - not null or undefined');
        }

        // 4. If mapfn is undefined, then let mapping be false.
        var mapFn = arguments.length > 1 ? arguments[1] : void undefined;
        var T;
        if (typeof mapFn !== 'undefined') {
          // 5. else
          // 5. a If IsCallable(mapfn) is false, throw a TypeError exception.
          if (!isCallable(mapFn)) {
            throw new TypeError('Array.from: when provided, the second argument must be a function');
          }

          // 5. b. If thisArg was supplied, let T be thisArg; else let T be undefined.
          if (arguments.length > 2) {
            T = arguments[2];
          }
        }

        // 10. Let lenValue be Get(items, "length").
        // 11. Let len be ToLength(lenValue).
        var len = toLength(items.length);

        // 13. If IsConstructor(C) is true, then
        // 13. a. Let A be the result of calling the [[Construct]] internal method 
        // of C with an argument list containing the single item len.
        // 14. a. Else, Let A be ArrayCreate(len).
        var A = isCallable(C) ? Object(new C(len)) : new Array(len);

        // 16. Let k be 0.
        var k = 0;
        // 17. Repeat, while k < len… (also steps a - h)
        var kValue;
        while (k < len) {
          kValue = items[k];
          if (mapFn) {
            A[k] = typeof T === 'undefined' ? mapFn(kValue, k) : mapFn.call(T, kValue, k);
          } else {
            A[k] = kValue;
          }
          k += 1;
        }
        // 18. Let putStatus be Put(A, "length", len, true).
        A.length = len;
        // 20. Return A.
        return A;
      };
    }());

    BOLD.common.eventEmitter.on('BOLD_COMMON_variant_changed', function(res) {
        var form = res.selector.form;

        $(form).find('.ro_widget').fadeOut(0);
    });

    BOLD.common.eventEmitter.on('BOLD_RECURRING_ORDERS_widget_loaded', function(res) {
        var form = res.data.form;
        var roWidget = form.querySelector('.ro_widget');
        $(roWidget).fadeOut(0);

        setTimeout(function() {
            var oneTime = form.querySelector('.bold-ro__one-time-div label');
            var subscription = form.querySelector('.bold-ro__recurring-div label');


            // Bind the events to the custom select box.
            var frequencyNum = form.querySelector('.bold-ro__frequency-num');
            var frequencyType = form.querySelector('.bold-ro__frequency-type');
            var frequencyTypeText = form.querySelector('.bold-ro__frequency-type-text');
            var customSelect = form.querySelector('#custom-select');

            if (frequencyNum && frequencyType && customSelect) {
                customSelect.addEventListener('change', function(e) {

                    switch(parseInt(e.target.value)) {
                        // 1 Week
                        case 0:
                            frequencyNum.value = 1;
                            frequencyType.value = 2;
                            frequencyTypeText.value = 'Week(s)';
                            break;

                        // 2 Weeks
                        case 1:
                            frequencyNum.value = 2;
                            frequencyType.value = 2;
                            frequencyTypeText.value = 'Week(s)';
                            break;

                        // Default Case is 1 Month
                        default:
                            frequencyNum.value = 1;
                            frequencyType.value = 3;
                            frequencyTypeText.value = 'Month(s)';
                    }
                    $(frequencyNum, frequencyType).trigger('change');
                });

                // Default option to 1 Month
                customSelect.value = 1;
                frequencyNum.value = 1;
                frequencyType.value = 3;
                frequencyTypeText.value = 'Month(s)';
                $(frequencyNum, frequencyType).trigger('change');

                // Bind the event for the subscription information modal
                var anchorLink = form.querySelector('.subscription-info');

                if (anchorLink) {
                    anchorLink.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        form.querySelector('.ro--tooltip').classList.add('visible');
                    });

                    form.querySelector('.ro--tooltip .ro--tooltip-content button').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        form.querySelector('.ro--tooltip').classList.remove('visible');
                    });

                    window.onclick = function(e) {
                        if (e.target == form.querySelector('.ro--tooltip')) {
                            form.querySelector('.ro--tooltip').classList.remove('visible');
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            $(roWidget).fadeIn(300);
        }, 100);
    });
</script>

<script>
  $(document).ready( function(){
    var $searchBlock = $('.search_block_mobile');

    var $searchTrigger = $searchBlock.find('.search-icon');
    var $form = $searchBlock.find('form');

    var $input = $searchBlock.find('.search_query');
    $form.hide();

    $searchTrigger.on('click', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();

      if ($form.hasClass('active')) {
        $form.removeClass('active');
        $form.fadeOut();
      } else {
        $form.addClass('active');

        $form.fadeIn(200);

        $input.focus();
        $input.click();
        $input.trigger('touchstart');
      }
    });
  });
</script>

  if ($(window).width() < 769) {

        var menuLink = mobileBottomBar.find('li.menu a');
        if (menuLink) {
          menuLink.click(function () {
            $("#navigation").data("navigation").toggleOffcanvas();
          });
        }
      }
    });
  }

<script type="text/javascript">
    var Shopify = Shopify || {};
    Shopify.optionsMap = {};
    Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector = function(selectorIndex) {
        switch (selectorIndex) {
            case 0:
                var key = 'root';
                var selector = jQuery('.single-option-selector:eq(0)');
                break;
            case 1:
                var key = jQuery('.single-option-selector:eq(0)').val();
                var selector = jQuery('.single-option-selector:eq(1)');
                break;
            case 2:
                var key = jQuery('.single-option-selector:eq(0)').val();  
                key += ' / ' + jQuery('.single-option-selector:eq(1)').val();
                var selector = jQuery('.single-option-selector:eq(2)');
        }
        var initialValue = selector.val();
        selector.empty();    
        var availableOptions = Shopify.optionsMap[key];
        for (var i=0; i<availableOptions.length; i++) {
            var option = availableOptions[i];
            var newOption = jQuery('<option></option>').val(option).html(option);
            selector.append(newOption);
        }
        jQuery('.swatch[data-option-index="' + selectorIndex + '"] .swatch-element').each(function() {
            if (jQuery.inArray($(this).attr('data-value'), availableOptions) !== -1) {
                $(this).removeClass('soldout').show().find(':radio').removeAttr('disabled','disabled').removeAttr('checked');
            }
            else {
                $(this).addClass('soldout').hide().find(':radio').removeAttr('checked').attr('disabled','disabled');
            }
        });
        if (jQuery.inArray(initialValue, availableOptions) !== -1) {
            selector.val(initialValue);
        }
        selector.trigger('change');  
    };
    Shopify.linkOptionSelectors = function(product) {
        // Building our mapping object.
        for (var i=0; i<product.variants.length; i++) {
            var variant = product.variants[i];
            if (variant.available) {
                // Gathering values for the 1st drop-down.
                Shopify.optionsMap['root'] = Shopify.optionsMap['root'] || [];
                Shopify.optionsMap['root'].push(variant.option1);
                Shopify.optionsMap['root'] = Shopify.uniq(Shopify.optionsMap['root']);
                // Gathering values for the 2nd drop-down.
                if (product.options.length > 1) {
                    var key = variant.option1;
                    Shopify.optionsMap[key] = Shopify.optionsMap[key] || [];
                    Shopify.optionsMap[key].push(variant.option2);
                    Shopify.optionsMap[key] = Shopify.uniq(Shopify.optionsMap[key]);
                }
                // Gathering values for the 3rd drop-down.
                if (product.options.length === 3) {
                    var key = variant.option1 + ' / ' + variant.option2;
                    Shopify.optionsMap[key] = Shopify.optionsMap[key] || [];
                    Shopify.optionsMap[key].push(variant.option3);
                    Shopify.optionsMap[key] = Shopify.uniq(Shopify.optionsMap[key]);
                }
            }
        }
        // Update options right away.
        Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector(0);
        if (product.options.length > 1) Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector(1);
        if (product.options.length === 3) Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector(2);
        // When there is an update in the first dropdown.
        jQuery(".single-option-selector:eq(0)").change(function() {
            Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector(1);
            if (product.options.length === 3) Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector(2);
            return true;
        });
        // When there is an update in the second dropdown.
        jQuery(".single-option-selector:eq(1)").change(function() {
            if (product.options.length === 3) Shopify.updateOptionsInSelector(2);
            return true;
        });
    };
</script>

  $('#CartTotal').on('change', function() {
	var fsb_bar = $('#fsb_bar');

    if(fsb_bar) {
      
      var cart_total = parseInt($(this).val());
      
      if (cart_total == 0) 
        fsb_bar.html('FREE Shipping and FREE Gift on all orders over <span id="fsb_symbol" style="color: rgb(255, 194, 41); font-family: Helvetica; font-size: 14px;">$</span><span id="fsb_amount" style="color: rgb(255, 194, 41); font-family: Helvetica; font-size: 14px;">99</span>');
      else if (cart_total <= 9900) 
        fsb_bar.html('Only <span id="fsb_symbol" style="color: rgb(255, 194, 41); font-family: Helvetica; font-size: 14px;">$</span><span id="fsb_amount" style="color: rgb(255, 194, 41); font-family: Helvetica; font-size: 14px;">' + ((9900 - cart_total)/100).toFixed(2) + '</span> away from FREE Shipping and FREE Gift!');
//    else if (cart_total <= 20000) 
//        fsb_bar.html('You have qualified for your FREE Gift or Spend  <span id="fsb_symbol" style="color: rgb(255, 194, 41); font-family: Helvetica; font-size: 14px; letter-spacing:0.05em;">$</span><span id="fsb_amount" style="color: rgb(255, 194, 41); font-family: Helvetica; font-size: 14px; letter-spacing:0.05em;">' + ((20000 - cart_total)/100).toFixed(2) + '</span> to upgrade to a Premium Gift!');
      else
        fsb_bar.html('Congratulations! You get FREE Shipping and a FREE Gift!');

    }
  });

<script>


function quickview_InitializeSwatches(product, selectedColor) {
    var productJson = product
    var variantsJson = product['variants'];
    var colors = [];
    var placeholder_image, originalColorSelector, optionPosition;

    $('.quick-view-product .color_swatches').remove();

    $.each(productJson.options, function (key, variant) {
        if(variant.name.toLowerCase() == 'color') {
            optionPosition = 'option' + (key + 1);
            originalColorSelector = $('select[data-option="' + optionPosition + '"]');
            originalColorSelector.parents('.selector-wrapper').hide()
            $('.quick-view-product .price-product-detail').after('<div class="color_swatches"><div>Color: <span class="color_selection"></span></div><div class="swatch_wrapper"></div></div>');
        }
    });

    if(optionPosition) {
        $.each(variantsJson, function(key, variant) {   
            if(variant[optionPosition].indexOf('(Bundle price)') == -1) {             
                colors.push(variant[optionPosition]);
            }
        }); 

        colors = colors.filter( onlyUnique );

        $.each(colors, function(key, color) {
            outputSwatches(color);
        }); 
        
        appendColon();
        setTimeout(function(){
            
            selectedColor = selectedColor.replace(/[\/]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z\s]/gi, '').trim();
            
            $('[data-swatch-color="' + selectedColor + '"]').click();
        }, 500);
    }

      $(document).on('click','.quick-view-product .color_swatch', function() {
        var selectedColor = $(this).data('swatch-color').toLowerCase();
        $('.color_selection').text(selectedColor);
        $('.color_swatch').removeClass('selected');
        $(this).addClass('selected');

        originalColorSelector.find('option').each(function() {
          var value = $(this).attr('value').replace(/[\/]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z\s]/gi, '').trim().toLowerCase();

          if(selectedColor == value) {
            originalColorSelector.val($(this).attr('value')).trigger('change');
            $('.quick-view-product select[name="id"]').change();
          }
        });

      });      

    $(document).on('change','.quick-view-product select[name="id"]', function() {
        var variantId = '?variant=' + $('select[name="id"]').find(":selected").attr('value');
        var imgHref = $('#product-featured-image-quickview').parent().data('original-link');

        $('#product-featured-image-quickview').parent().attr('href', imgHref + variantId);
    });
}


function productInitializeSwatches() {
    var productJson = null
    var variantsJson = null;
    var colors = [];
    var placeholder_image, originalColorSelector, optionPosition;


    $.each(productJson.options, function (key, variant) {

      if(variant.toLowerCase() == 'color') {
        optionPosition = 'option' + (key + 1);
        originalColorSelector = $('select[data-option="' + optionPosition + '"]');
        originalColorSelector.parents('.selector-wrapper').hide()
        $('.proVariants').prepend('<div class="color_swatches"><div>Color: <span class="color_selection"></span></div><div class="swatch_wrapper"></div></div>');
      }
    });

    if(optionPosition) {
      $.each(variantsJson, function(key, variant) {
        if(variant[optionPosition].indexOf('(Bundle price)') == -1) {
            colors.push(variant[optionPosition]);
        }
      }); 

      colors = colors.filter( onlyUnique );

      $.each(colors, function(key, color) {
        outputSwatches(color);
      }); 

      appendColon();
        setTimeout(function(){
            var selectedColor = originalColorSelector.find(":selected").text().replace(/[\/]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z\s]/gi, '').trim().toLowerCase();
            selectedColor = selectedColor.substr(0,1).toUpperCase()+selectedColor.substr(1);
            $('[data-swatch-color="' + selectedColor + '"]').click();
        }, 500);    
    }

      $(document).on('click','.color_swatch', function() {
        var selectedColor = $(this).data('swatch-color');
        $('.color_selection').text(selectedColor);
        $('.color_swatch').removeClass('selected');
        $(this).addClass('selected');

        originalColorSelector.find('option').each(function() {
          var value = $(this).attr('value').replace(/[\/]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z\s]/gi, '').replace(/\s/g, '').toLowerCase();
          value = value.substr(0,1).toUpperCase()+value.substr(1);

          if(selectedColor == value) {
            originalColorSelector.val($(this).attr('value')).trigger('change');
          }
        });
        
      });  
      

}


 /*===========================================================================
      # global functions
      ===========================================================================*/

    function outputSwatches(color) {
        var swatch;
        var formattedColor = color.replace(/[^a-z\s]/gi, '').replace(/ /g, '_').replace(' ', '_').toLowerCase();
        var color = color.replace(/[\/]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z\s]/gi, '').trim().toLowerCase();
        color = color.substr(0,1).toUpperCase()+color.substr(1);

        formattedColor = formattedColor.replace('__', '_')
        formattedColor = formattedColor.substr(0,1).toUpperCase()+formattedColor.substr(1);

        if(formattedColor.charAt( formattedColor.length-1 ) == "_") {
            formattedColor = formattedColor.slice(0, -1);
        }

        placeholder_image = "//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1121/1398/t/268/assets/logo_small.png?135198";
        placeholder_image = placeholder_image.replace('logo', formattedColor); 

        $('.swatch_wrapper').append('<img data-swatch-color="'+ color +'"  src="' + placeholder_image + '" class="color_swatch" />');
        
        swatch = $('img[data-swatch-color="'+ color +'"]');
        
    }

      
      
      function appendColon() {
        $('.selector-wrapper label').each(function() {
          $(this).text($(this).text() + ':');
        });
      }

      function onlyUnique(value, index, self) { 
        return self.indexOf(value) === index;
      }


</script>
